- **spectral-feature-extraction-and-annotation**: Use when you have raw LC/MS data in mzML format and need to perform non-targeted screening to discover unknown chemical features without a predefined list of target compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-feature-extraction-and-annotation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-feature-extraction-and-integration**: Use when when you have a base message passing neural network (e.g., chemprop) trained on molecular graphs and need to augment it with infrared spectral information to improve prediction accuracy for molecular properties. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-feature-extraction-and-integration) · ★15)
- **spectral-feature-normalization**: Use when when you have raw LC-MS metabolomics data in .mzML or .npy format from multiple disease groups with varying ionization efficiencies or detector sensitivities, and you need to train a deep learning model for disease classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-feature-normalization) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-feature-scrambling-permutation**: Use when when performing large-scale untargeted metabolomics annotations where you need to assess the false discovery rate of metabolite identifications but lack sufficient negative control samples or decoy compound databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-feature-scrambling-permutation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-feature-standardization**: Use when after peak-picking stage completes on centroided mzML or netCDF raw LC-MS data via any of the three wrapped algorithms (Centwave, FeatureFinderMetabo, ADAP), when you need to pass the detected features to downstream SLAW stages (alignment, isotope/adduct grouping, gap-filling, MS2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-feature-standardization) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-feature-table-generation**: Use when you have raw LC-MS data in mzXML format (or vendor formats convertible via MS-Convert) and need to identify and quantify metabolic features before multi-sample alignment. Use MS1 peak picking for full-scan or DDA data to extract Gaussian and non-Gaussian shaped peaks; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-feature-table-generation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
